[Ondine's Fountain]
The source of the spring's fresh water is here, the liquid bubbling up from deep within the womb of the world. A beautiful naiad sits on a large, flat rock, bathing in the warmth of the sun as it filters through the metallic foliage of the golden oak. Her hair a deep blue and her skin a dozen shades paler. Within her eyes, the observant might notice a swirling vortex of turquoise and cerulean, like the meeting of sky and sea. A few exotic trinkets and useful items have been laid out on the rock around her, as if she were seeking to set up a shop.

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

[The Lonely Moon: Stars Club, Foyer]
The doors open into a quaint foyer with heavily polished cherrywood floors. Elegantly carved ebonwood pillars rise from the floor at the corners where the walls, papered in murals of detailed depictions of the night sky and its constellations, come together. A cheerily lit side room to the west houses a myriad of unique clothing on mannequins, teak hangers, and cedarwood shelves, as well as glass counters filled with sparkling jewels and baubles. The eastern archway from the foyer leads into a cramped post office, dominated by a large counter in front of a vast array of tiny envelope cabinets.

[The Lonely Moon: Stars Club, Tavern]
Behind the heavy oak door, the dimly-lit room opens into a spacious tavern full of glass-topped, lacquered wooden tables and cushioned chairs. A beautifully polished bar, carved with star and tree shapes, is set against the northern wall. Behind the bar top, bottles of liquor, kegs of beer, and all manner of alcoholic beverage are lined up, tapped and waiting for patrons. High-backed stools are set against the bar, allowing patrons to mingle with one another. The cacophonous sounds of talking, laughing, and whispering fill the packed room. The hearty aroma of well-cooked food wafts from the kitchen entrance behind the bar. Servers and bartenders move swiftly back and forth from the tavern to the kitchen. A small servant door is visible behind the bar. A T-shaped stage is centered at the eastern wall, and though the barmaids and barkeeps continue to move quickly, refilling drinks or bringing food in and out from the kitchen behind the bar, a reverent silence spreads over the customers as entertainers take the stage. Portraits of various famous adventurers decorate the walls, yet the most pronounced portrait of the owner has been hung behind the bar.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: open heavy oak door

[The Lonely Moon: Stars Club, Foyer]
The doors open into a quaint foyer with heavily polished cherrywood floors. Elegantly carved ebonwood pillars rise from the floor at the corners where the walls, papered in murals of detailed depictions of the night sky and its constellations, come together. A dimly lit, diamond and platinum-dusted chandelier hangs from the darkened ceiling like a bundle of shooting stars. Further down the corridor from the foyer is a heavy oak door, studded with silver stars and a lone, crescent moon painted in the center. A burly duskwen guards the back door. A cheerily lit side room to the west houses a myriad of unique clothing on mannequins, teak hangers, and cedarwood shelves, as well as glass counters filled with sparkling jewels and baubles. The eastern archway from the foyer leads into a cramped post office, dominated by a large counter in front of a vast array of tiny envelope cabinets.

The area is illuminated.

[Dagurne's Archery and Fletching]
A very frail-looking, graying old Goblin ranger sits comfortably on the log of an old elm tree. He has piles of different types of wood neatly stacked around him, and with his deft, agile hands, he fletches arrows in masterfully straight shafts. A small fire and a kettle of water sit to one side of him; he occasionally pours water over a thick flat stone from the fire, creating steam. He then shapes the wood for bows, or works out the knobby parts of arrows. Wood shavings, sandstones, sinews and cordage for bowstrings, and polishing rags lay all around him. Finished products are stacked carefully on one of the crumbling sides of the old building which he has set his shop in.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

The area is illuminated.

[Double Boil]

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.

The area is illuminated.

[Merchant Square]
A small sign has been set in front of the store fronts, listing the nearby shops: Malinar's Magic and Talafon's Weapons. Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained square. Through the open facade, young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small, but functional sign reads, "Malinar's Magic and Mayhem".

[Urda Town Square Center]
On this site, a fierce battle for control of the great city was waged, and on this site is where its citizens now celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways that scatter over the square. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. The many languages of races and guilds reverberate off the storefronts of the surrounding Merchant Square where a constant foot traffic ebbs and flows, making this the heart of the city.
